activation_id=?',id));\n const activationView=(r:Row):RuntimeActivationView=>({activationId:String(r.activation_id),sessionId:String(r.session_id),state:String(r.state),tokensUsed:Number(r.tokens_used),tokenLimit:Number(r.token_limit),maxSteps:Number(r.max_steps),unknownUsage:r.unknown_usage===1,configHash:String(r.config_hash)});\n const tool=(id:string,activationId:string)=>required(get('SELECT * FROM runtime_tool_calls WHERE operation_id=? AND activation_id=?',id,activationId));\n const toolView=(r:Row):RuntimeToolView=>({operationId:String(r.operation_id),activationId:String(r.activation_id),sessionId:String(r.session_id),stepNo:Number(r.step_no),toolCallId:String(r.tool_call_id),toolName:String(required(get('SELECT tool_name FROM tool_operations WHERE operation_id=?',String(r.operation_id))).tool_name),state:String(r.state),resultMessageId:r.result_message_id===null?null:String(r.result_message_id),argsHash:String(r.args_hash),errorCode:r.error_code===null?null:String(r.error_code)});\n function host(c:{endpointId:string;taskId:string;workerId:string;claimToken:string},recovery=false){\n  const row=required(get('SELECT a.*,w.state accepted_state,w.activation_id,w.worker_id,w.claim_token,p.owner_id,r.run_epoch,r.control_gate,r.terminal_outcome,x.work_epoch FROM accepted_work w JOIN a2a_tasks t ON t.endpoint_id=w.endpoint_id AND t.task_id=w.task_id JOIN attempts a ON a.attempt_id=t.attempt_id JOIN runs r ON r.run_id=a.run_id JOIN projects p ON p.project_id=r.project_id JOIN works x ON x.run_id=a.run_id AND x.work_id=a.work_id WHERE w.endpoint_id=? AND w.task_id=?',c.endpointId,c.taskId));\n  if(!(recovery?['ACTIVE','RECONCILE','FINISHED'].includes(String(row.accepted_state)):row.accepted_state==='ACTIVE')||row.worker_id!==c.workerId||row.claim_token!==c.claimToken)reject('EXECUTION_HOST_CLAIM_CHANGED','A durable original consumer claim is required',403);\n  if(recovery){const member=get('SELECT m.role FROM project_members m JOIN runs r ON r.project_id=m.project_id WHERE r.run_id=? AND task_id=?',c.endpointId,c.taskId)?.cancel_requested===1)reject('EXECUTION_CANCELED','Task cancellation closes action admission',403);\n  return row;\n }\n function permit(row:Row){\n  const g=required(get('SELECT * FROM execution_grants WHERE attempt_id=? AND activation_id=?',String(row.attempt_id),String(row.activation_id))),p=JSON.parse(String(g.permit_json)) as Record<string,unknown>;\n  if(row.control_gate!=='OPEN'||row.terminal_outcome!==null||row.ended_at!==null||g.status!=='ACTIVE'||String(g.expires_at)<=now()||String(p.notBefore)>now()||p.runEpoch!==row.run_epoch||p.workEpoch!==row.work_epoch)reject('EXECUTION_PERMIT_INVALID','Run control, epoch or grant changed',403);\n  return {g,p};\n }\n function assertActive(id:string,reserved=0){\n  const a=activation(id);if(a.state!=='RUNNING')reject('RUNTIME_NOT_RUNNING','Runtime cannot admit new actions');\n  const h=host({endpointId:String(a.endpoint_id),taskId:String(a.task_id),workerId:String(a.worker_id),claimToken:String(a.claim_token)});permit(h);\n  if(a.unknown_usage===1||Number(a.tokens_used)+reserved>Number(a.token_limit)||Number(a.tokens_used)>=Number(a.token_limit))reject('MODEL_BUDGET_EXCEEDED','Token budget exhausted or prior model cost is unknown');\n  if(reserved>0&&Number(get('SELECT count(*) n FROM runtime_model_accounting WHERE activation_id=?',id)?.n??0)>=Number(a.max_steps))reject('MODEL_STEP_LIMIT','Activation step budget exhausted');\n  return a;\n }\n const handlers={\n  getRuntimeWorkspaceRecovery(input:RuntimeInput<'getRuntimeWorkspaceRecovery'>){return transaction(db,()=>{const h=host(input,true),a=activation(String(h.activation_id)),g=required(get('SELECT * FROM execution_grants WHERE grant_id=?',String(a.grant_id))),work=required(get('SELECT definition_json FROM work_revisions WHERE run_id=? basis={runEpoch:Number(run.run_epoch),planRevision:i.planRevision,requirementRevision:i.requirementRevision,planHash:i.reviewHash,requirementSha256:digest(String(requirement.content_json)),sourceAttemptId:i.sourceAttemptId,sourceVerificationId:String(source.operation_id),round,issues,carriedResults:carries};\n run.current_plan_revision===row.published_plan_revision&&run.current_requirement_revision===json<{requirementRevision:number}>(row.basis_json).requirementRevision;return as string|null};}\n const handlers={\n  prepareVerifiedRepair(i:Prepare){return transaction(db,()=>{const hash=digest(canonical(i)),old=get('SELECT * FROM verified_repairs WHERE   const fix:Draft['works'][number]={workId:fixId,workRevision:1,role:'developer',goal:'Repair independently verified findings from '+f.sourceWork.workId+'; preserve the confirmed requirements and parsed=parseContract(verifiedRepairRequests[method],input);return (handlers[method] as (i:unknown)=>unknown)(parsed);},\n };\n}\n","import { randomUUID } from 'node:crypto';\nimport type { DatabaseSync, SQLInputValue } from 'node:sqlite';\nimport { parseContract } from '@devmesh/contracts';\nimport { StorageError } from './errors.js';\nimport { canonical } from './json.js';\nimport { digest, transaction } from './migrations.js';\nimport { sessionRequests } from './session-protocol.js';\nimport type { SessionMethod, SessionInput, ObjectWrite, ObjectTarget, SessionInfo, SessionMessage, ContentInfo, ModelStep, ContentReference } from './session-protocol.js';\n\nimport {createCandidateMaterialDomain} from './candidate-material-domain.js';\nimport {createWorkspacePreservationDomain} from './workspace-preservation-domain.js';\nimport {createQualityDomain} from './quality-domain.js';\nimport {createWorkVerificationDomain} from './work-verification-domain.js';\nimport {createReleaseDeliveryDomain} from './release-delivery-domain.js';\nimport {createVerifiedRepairDomain} from './verified-repair-domain.js';\n\ntype Row = Record<string, string | number | null>;\nconst uid = (prefix: string) => `${prefix}_${randomUUID().replaceAll('-', '')}`;\nconst now = () => new Date().toISOString();\nconst reject = (code: string, message: string, status = 409): never => { throw new StorageError(code, status, message); };\n\nexport function createSessionDomain(db: DatabaseSync) {\n  const candidateMaterials=createCandidateMaterialDomain(db);\n  const workspacePreservations=createWorkspacePreservationDomain(db);\n  const quality=createQualityDomain(db);\n  const workVerifications=createWorkVerificationDomain(db);\n  const verifiedRepairs=createVerifiedRepairDomain(db);\n  const releaseDeliveries=createReleaseDeliveryDomain(db);\n  const get = (sql: string, ...args: SQLInputValue[]) => db.prepare(sql).get(...args) as Row | undefined;\n  const all = (sql: string, ...args: SQLInputValue[]) => db.prepare(sql).all(...args) as Row[];\n  const exec = (sql: string, ...args: SQLInputValue[]) => db.prepare(sql).run(...args);\n  const required = (row: Row | undefined): Row => row ?? reject('RESOURCE_NOT_FOUND', 'Requested storage record does not exist', 404);\n  const session = (principalId: string, sessionId: string) => {\n    const row = required(get('SELECT s.*,p.owner_id FROM agent_sessions s JOIN runs r ON r.run_id=s.run_id JOIN projects p ON p.project_id=r.project_id WHERE s.session_id=?', sessionId));\n    if (row.owner_id !== principalId) reject('FORBIDDEN', 'Session belongs to another principal', 403);\n    return row;\n  };\n  const sessionView = (r: Row): SessionInfo => ({ sessionId: String(r.session_id), runId: String(r.run_id), attemptId: String(r.attempt_id), roleSnapshotId: String(r.role_snapshot_id), lastMessageSeq: Number(r.last_message_seq), lastStepNo: Number(r.last_step_no), status: String(r.status) });\n  const contentView = (r: Row): ContentInfo => ({ contentHash: String(r.content_hash), byteSize: Number(r.byte_size), storageKey: String(r.storage_key), mimeType: String(r.mime_type), state: String(r.state) });\n  const messageView = (r: Row): SessionMessage => ({ messageId: String(r.message_id), seq: Number(r.message_seq), sessionId: String(r.session_id), activationId: String(r.activation_id), stepNo: r.step_no as number | null, kind: String(r.kind), contentHash: String(r.body_content_hash), toolCallId: r.tool_call_id as string | null, sourceRef: r.source_ref ? JSON.parse(String(r.source_ref)) : null, createdAt: String(r.created_at) });\n  const stepView = (r: Row): ModelStep => ({ sessionId: String(r.session_id), stepNo: Number(r.step_no), state: String(r.state), requestHash: String(r.request_content_hash), responseHash: r.response_content_hash as string | null, contextSnapshotId: String(r.context_snapshot_id), usage: r.usage_json ? JSON.parse(String(r.usage_json)) : null, providerRequestId: r.provider_request_id as string | null });\n  const ready = (hash: string) => {\n    const row = required(get('SELECT * FROM content_objects WHERE content_hash=?', hash));\n    if (row.state !== 'READY') reject('CONTENT_UNAVAILABLE', 'Content is missing or unavailable', 503);\n    return row;\n  };\n  const write = (principalId: string, writeId: string) => {\n    const row = required(get('SELECT w.*,o.result_json FROM object_write_operations w JOIN operations o ON o.operation_id=w.operation_id WHERE write_id=?', writeId));\n    if (row.owner_id !== principalId) reject('FORBIDDEN', 'Write belongs to another principal', 403);\n    if(row.purpose==='workspacePreservation')workspacePreservations.authorize(principalId,JSON.parse(String(row.target_json)));\n    if(row.purpose==='candidateMaterial')candidateMaterials.authorize(principalId,JSON.parse(String(row.target_json)));\n    if(row.purpose==='qualityMaterial')quality.authorize(principalId,JSON.parse(String(row.target_json)));\n    if(row.purpose==='workVerification')workVerifications.authorize(principalId,JSON.parse(String(row.target_json)));\n    if(row.purpose==='releaseDelivery')releaseDeliveries.authorize(principalId,JSON.parse(String(row.target_json)));\n    if(row.purpose==='verifiedRepair')verifiedRepairs.authorize(principalId,JSON.parse(String(row.target_json)));\n    return row;\n  };\n  const writeView = (r: Row): ObjectWrite => ({ writeId: String(r.write_id), operationId: String(r.operation_id), ownerId: String(r.owner_id), expectedHash: String(r.expected_hash), expectedSize: Number(r.expected_size), mimeType: String(r.mime_type), target: JSON.parse(String(r.target_json)), stagingKey: String(r.staging_key), state: String(r.state), result: r.result_json ? JSON.parse(String(r.result_json)) : null });\n  function targetSession(principalId: string, target: ObjectTarget): Row | undefined {\n    if (target.kind === 'content' || target.kind === 'attachment') return;\n    if(target.kind==='workVerification'){workVerifications.authorize(principalId,target);return;}\n    if(target.kind==='releaseDelivery'){releaseDeliveries.authorize(principalId,target);return;}\n    if(target.kind==='verifiedRepair'){verifiedRepairs.authorize(principalId,target);return;}\n    if(target.kind==='qualityMaterial'){quality.authorize(principalId,target);return;}\n    if(target.kind==='workspacePreservation'){workspacePreservations.authorize(principalId,target);return;}\n    if(target.kind==='candidateMaterial'){candidateMaterials.authorize(principalId,target);return;}\n    return session(principalId, target.kind === 'context' ? target.manifest.sessionId : target.sessionId);\n  }\n  function appendMessage(s: Row, input: { clientMessageId: string; activationId: string; messageKind: string; stepNo?: number | undefined; toolCallId?: string | undefined; sourceRef?: { messageId: string } | undefined }, hash: string): Record<string, unknown> {\n    const old = get('SELECT * FROM session_messages WHERE session_id=? AND client_message_id=?', s.session_id!, input.clientMessageId);\n    if (old) {\n      if (old.body_content_hash !== hash || old.kind !== input.messageKind || old.activation_id !== input.activationId || old.step_no !== (input.stepNo ?? null) || old.tool_call_id !== (input.toolCallId ?? null) || old.source_ref !== (input.sourceRef ? canonical(input.sourceRef) : null)) reject('MESSAGE_KEY_REUSED', 'Message key was used with another body or metadata');\n      return { messageId: old.message_id, seq: old.message_seq, sessionId: s.session_id };\n    }\n    if (s.status !== 'OPEN') reject('SESSION_CLOSED', 'Cannot append to a closed session');\n    const tool = input.messageKind === 'tool-call' || input.messageKind === 'tool-result';\n    if (tool !== (input.toolCallId !== undefined)) reject('TOOL_CALL_ID_INVALID', 'Tool messages require a toolCallId', 422);\n    if (tool && (!input.stepNo || !get('SELECT step_no FROM model_steps WHERE session_id=? AND step_no=? AND response_content_hash IS NOT NULL', s.session_id!, input.stepNo))) reject('MODEL_RESPONSE_REQUIRED', 'Persist the complete model response before tool messages');\n    if (input.sourceRef && !get('SELECT message_id FROM session_messages WHERE session_id=? AND message_id=?', s.session_id!, input.sourceRef.messageId)) reject('SOURCE_SCOPE_INVALID', 'Source message must belong to the same session');\n    const messageId = uid('message'), seq = Number(s.last_message_seq)+1;\n    exec('INSERT INTO session_messages VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)', messageId, s.session_id!, seq, input.clientMessageId, input.activationId, input.stepNo ?? null, input.messageKind, hash, input.toolCallId ?? null, input.sourceRef ? canonical(input.sourceRef) : null, now());\n    exec('UPDATE agent_sessions SET last_message_seq=? WHERE session_id=?', seq, s.session_id!);\n    return { messageId, seq, sessionId: s.session_id };\n  }\n  function commitTarget(principalId: string, target: ObjectTarget, hash: string, row: Row): Record<string, unknown> {\n    if (target.kind === 'content') return { contentHash: hash };\n    if(target.kind==='workVerification')return workVerifications.commit(principalId,target,hash,String(row.write_id));\n    if(target.kind==='releaseDelivery')return releaseDeliveries.commit(principalId,target,hash,String(row.write_id));\n    if(target.kind==='verifiedRepair')return verifiedRepairs.commit(principalId,target,hash,String(row.write_id));\n    if(target.kind==='qualityMaterial')return quality.commit(principalId,target,hash,String(row.write_id));\n    if(target.kind==='workspacePreservation')return workspacePreservations.commit(principalId,target,hash,String(row.write_id));\n    if(target.kind==='candidateMaterial')return candidateMaterials.commit(principalId,target,hash,String(row.write_id));\n    if (target.kind === 'attachment') {\n      const old = get('SELECT * FROM attachments WHERE owner_id=? AND client_attachment_id=?', principalId, target.clientAttachmentId);\n      if (old) {\n        if (old.content_hash !== hash || old.name !== target.name || old.state !== 'READY') reject('ATTACHMENT_KEY_REUSED', 'Attachment key belongs to different content');\n        return { attachmentId: old.attachment_id, contentHash: hash };\n      }\n      const attachmentId = uid('attachment');\n      exec(\"INSERT INTO attachments VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,?,?,'READY',?)\", attachmentId, principalId, target.clientAttachmentId, row.operation_id!, target.name, row.expected_size!, hash, now());\n      return { attachmentId, contentHash: hash };\n    }\n    const s = targetSession(principalId, target)!;\n    if (s.status !== 'OPEN') reject('SESSION_CLOSED', 'Session is closed');\n    if (target.kind === 'message') return appendMessage(s, { ...target, sourceRef: target.sourceRef }, hash);\n    if (target.kind === 'context') {\n      const m = target.manifest;\n      if (digest(canonical(m)) !== hash) reject('CONTEXT_HASH_INVALID', 'Context bytes must equal the canonical manifest', 422);\n      if (m.roleSnapshotId !== s.role_snapshot_id || m.throughMessageSeq > Number(s.last_message_seq)) reject('CONTEXT_BASIS_CHANGED', 'Context role or cursor does not belong to this session');\n      let previous = 0;\n      for (const ref of m.messages) {\n        if (ref.seq <= previous || ref.seq > m.throughMessageSeq || !get('SELECT message_id FROM session_messages WHERE session_id=? AND message_id=? AND message_seq=? AND body_content_hash=?', s.session_id!, ref.messageId, ref.seq, ref.sha256)) reject('CONTEXT_BASIS_CHANGED', 'Context message order/hash differs from committed history');\n        ready(ref.sha256); previous = ref.seq;\n      }\n      if (m.summaryContentHash) {\n        if (!get(\"SELECT message_id FROM session_messages WHERE session_id=? AND body_content_hash=? AND kind='context-summary' AND message_seq<=?\", s.session_id!, m.summaryContentHash, m.throughMessageSeq)) reject('CONTEXT_SUMMARY_UNAUTHORIZED', 'Summary must be a saved summary in this session');\n        ready(m.summaryContentHash);\n      } else if (m.messages.length !== m.throughMessageSeq) reject('CONTEXT_MESSAGES_MISSING', 'Uncompressed context must list all messages through its cursor');\n      const id = uid('context');\n      exec('INSERT INTO context_snapshots VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)', id, s.session_id!, m.throughMessageSeq, hash, m.summaryContentHash ?? null, m.configHash, canonical(m.messages), now());\n      exec('UPDATE agent_sessions SET context_manifest_ref=? WHERE session_id=?', hash, s.session_id!);\n      return { contextSnapshotId: id, sessionId: s.session_id, contentHash: hash, throughMessageSeq: m.throughMessageSeq };\n    }\n    if (target.kind === 'modelRequest') {\n      const snapshot = required(get('SELECT * FROM context_snapshots WHERE workflow_session_id=? AND context_snapshot_id=?', s.session_id!, target.contextSnapshotId));\n      if (snapshot.config_hash !== target.configHash) reject('CONTEXT_BASIS_CHANGED', 'Model request config differs from context');\n      ready(String(snapshot.manifest_content_hash));\n      if (target.stepNo !== Number(s.last_step_no)+1 || get(\"SELECT step_no FROM model_steps WHERE session_id=? AND state IN ('PREPARED','SENT')\", s.session_id!)) reject('MODEL_STEP_ORDER_INVALID', 'Previous model step must be resolved and step numbers contiguous');\n      exec(\"INSERT INTO model_steps(session_id,step_no,context_snapshot_id,request_content_hash,state,started_at) VALUES(?,?,?,?,'PREPARED',?)\", s.session_id!, target.stepNo, target.contextSnapshotId, hash, now());\n      exec('UPDATE agent_sessions SET last_step_no=? WHERE session_id=?', target.stepNo, s.session_id!);\n      return { sessionId: s.session_id, stepNo: target.stepNo, state: 'PREPARED' };\n    }\n    const step = required(get('SELECT * FROM model_steps WHERE session_id=? AND step_no=?', s.session_id!, target.stepNo));\n    if (step.response_content_hash !== null) reject('MODEL_RESPONSE_IMMUTABLE', 'A step response already exists');\n    exec('UPDATE model_steps SET response_content_hash=?,usage_json=?,provider_request_id=?,state=?,ended_at=? WHERE session_id=? AND step_no=?', hash, target.usage ? canonical(target.usage) : null, target.providerRequestId ?? null, target.state, now(), s.session_id!, target.stepNo);\n    const message = appendMessage(s, { clientMessageId: `model-response.${target.stepNo}`, activationId: target.activationId, stepNo: target.stepNo, messageKind: 'assistant' }, hash);\n    return { ...message, stepNo: target.stepNo, state: target.state };\n  }\n  function resolveReference(principalId: string, ref: ContentReference): Row {\n    let row: Row;\n    if(ref.kind==='releaseDelivery'){row=releaseDeliveries.resolve(principalId,ref);\n    }else if(ref.kind==='verifiedRepair'){row=verifiedRepairs.resolve(principalId,ref);\n    }else if(ref.kind==='workVerification'){row=workVerifications.resolve(principalId,ref);\n    }else if(ref.kind==='qualityMaterial'){row=quality.resolve(principalId,ref);\n    }else if(ref.kind==='workspacePreservation'){row=workspacePreservations.resolve(principalId,ref);\n    }else if(ref.kind==='candidateMaterial'){row=candidateMaterials.resolve(principalId,ref);\n    } else if (ref.kind === 'attachment') {\n      row = required(get(\"SELECT content_hash,owner_id FROM attachments WHERE attachment_id=? AND state='READY'\", ref.attachmentId));\n      if (row.owner_id !== principalId && !get('SELECT 1 FROM input_message_attachments a JOIN input_messages m ON m.message_id=a.message_id JOIN project_members p ON p.project_id=m.project_id WHERE a.attachment_id=? AND p.principal_id=?',ref.attachmentId,principalId)) reject('FORBIDDEN', 'Attachment belongs to another principal', 403);\n    } else if (ref.kind === 'artifact') {\n      row = required(get(\"SELECT a.content_hash,a.project_id,p.owner_id FROM artifacts a JOIN projects p ON p.project_id=a.project_id WHERE artifact_id=? AND a.state='READY'\", ref.artifactId));\n      if (!get('SELECT 1 FROM project_members WHERE project_id=? AND principal_id=?',row.project_id!,principalId)) reject('FORBIDDEN', 'Artifact belongs to another principal', 403);\n    } else if(ref.kind==='inputMessage'){\n      row=required(get(\"SELECT a.content_hash FROM input_messages i JOIN artifacts a ON a.artifact_id=i.body_artifact_id JOIN project_members m ON m.project_id=i.project_id AND m.principal_id=? WHERE i.message_id=? AND a.state='READY'\",principalId,ref.messageId));\n    } else if(ref.kind==='preparationInput'||ref.kind==='preparationResponse'){\n      const column=ref.kind==='preparationInput'?'p.input_hash':'s.response_hash';\n      row=required(get(`SELECT ${column} AS content_hash FROM preparation_attempts p JOIN preparation_jobs j ON j.job_id=p.job_id JOIN runs r ON r.run_id=p.run_id JOIN project_members m ON m.project_id=r.project_id AND m.principal_id=j.principal_id LEFT JOIN preparation_steps s ON s.preparation_attempt_id=p.preparation_attempt_id WHERE p.preparation_attempt_id=? AND j.principal_id=? AND ${column} IS NOT NULL`,ref.preparationAttemptId,principalId));\n    } else if (ref.kind === 'message') {\n      row = required(get('SELECT session_id,body_content_hash AS content_hash FROM session_messages WHERE message_id=?', ref.messageId));\n      session(principalId, String(row.session_id));\n    } else if (ref.kind === 'context') {\n      row = required(get('SELECT workflow_session_id AS session_id,manifest_content_hash AS content_hash FROM context_snapshots WHERE context_snapshot_id=?', ref.contextSnapshotId));\n      session(principalId, String(row.session_id));\n    } else {\n      session(principalId, ref.sessionId);\n      row = required(get(`SELECT ${ref.kind === 'modelRequest' ? 'request_content_hash' : 'response_content_hash'} AS content_hash FROM model_steps WHERE session_id=? AND step_no=?`, ref.sessionId, ref.stepNo));\n      if (!row.content_hash) reject('CONTENT_UNAVAILABLE', 'Step has no complete response yet', 409);\n    }\n    return ready(String(row.content_hash));\n  }\n  function unreferenced(hash: string) {\n    if (get(\"SELECT write_id FROM object_write_operations WHERE expected_hash=? AND state IN ('RECEIVING','SEALED')\", hash) || get(\"SELECT pin_id FROM storage_pins WHERE content_hash=? AND state='ACTIVE'\", hash)) reject('CONTENT_REFERENCED', 'Content has an active write or read pin');\n    const quote = (s: string) => '\"' + s.replaceAll('\"', '\"\"') + '\"';\n    for (const table of all(\"SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table' AND name NOT LIKE 'sqlite_%' AND name<>'storage_pins'\")) {\n      for (const key of all(`PRAGMA foreign_key_list(${quote(String(table.name))})`)) {\n        if (key.table === 'content_objects' && get(`SELECT 1 FROM ${quote(String(table.name))} WHERE ${quote(String(key.from))}=? LIMIT 1`, hash)) reject('CONTENT_REFERENCED', 'Content still has a business reference');\n      }\n    }\n  }\n  const handlers = {\n    beginObjectWrite(input: SessionInput<'beginObjectWrite'>) {\n      return transaction(db, () => {\n        targetSession(input.meta.principalId, input.target);\n        if(input.target.kind==='workspacePreservation')workspacePreservations.validateWrite({...input,target:input.target});\n        if(input.target.kind==='candidateMaterial')candidateMaterials.validateWrite({...input,target:input.target});\n        if(input.target.kind==='qualityMaterial')quality.validateWrite({...input,target:input.target});\n        if(input.target.kind==='workVerification')workVerifications.validateWrite({...input,target:input.target});\n        if(input.target.kind==='releaseDelivery')releaseDeliveries.validateWrite({...input,target:input.target});\n        if(input.target.kind==='verifiedRepair')verifiedRepairs.validateWrite({...input,target:input.target});\n        const { meta, ...payload } = input, requestHash = digest(canonical({ kind: 'WriteObject', payload }));\n        const old = get('SELECT operation_id,request_hash FROM operations WHERE principal_id=? AND scope_id=? AND idempotency_key=?', meta.principalId, meta.scopeId, meta.idempotencyKey);\n        if (old) {\n          if (old.request_hash !== requestHash) reject('IDEMPOTENCY_KEY_REUSED', 'Original write key belongs to different bytes or target');\n          return writeView(write(meta.principalId, String(required(get('SELECT write_id FROM object_write_operations WHERE operation_id=?', old.operation_id!)).write_id)));\n        }\n        const operationId = uid('op'), writeId = uid('write'), time = now();\n        exec(\"INSERT INTO operations VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,?,'ACCEPTED',NULL,?,?)\", operationId, meta.principalId, meta.scopeId, meta.idempotencyKey, requestHash, 'WriteObject', time, time);\n        exec(\"INSERT INTO object_write_operations VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,'RECEIVING',NULL,?,?)\", writeId, operationId, meta.principalId, meta.scopeId, input.target.kind, `objects/${writeId}.part`, input.expectedHash, input.expectedSize, input.mimeType, canonical(input.target), new Date(Date.now()+300000).toISOString(), time, time);\n        exec('UPDATE operations SET result_json=? WHERE operation_id=?', canonical({ writeId }), operationId);\n        return writeView(write(meta.principalId, writeId));\n      });\n    },\n    getObjectWrite(input: SessionInput<'getObjectWrite'>) { return writeView(write(input.principalId, input.writeId)); },\n    sealObjectWrite(input: SessionInput<'sealObjectWrite'>) {\n      return transaction(db, () => {\n        const row = write(input.principalId, input.writeId);\n        if (row.state !== 'REGISTERED') exec(\"UPDATE object_write_operations SET state='SEALED',error_json=NULL,updated_at=? WHERE write_id=?\", now(), input.writeId);\n        return writeView(write(input.principalId, input.writeId));\n      });\n    },\n    commitObjectWrite(input: SessionInput<'commitObjectWrite'>) {\n      return transaction(db, () => {\n        const row = write(input.principalId, input.writeId);\n        if (row.state === 'REGISTERED') return writeView(row);\n        if (row.state !== 'SEALED') reject('OBJECT_NOT_PUBLISHED', 'Verified publication must precede reference commit');\n        const hash = String(row.expected_hash), key = `sha256/${hash.slice(0,2)}/${hash}`, time = now();\n        const old = get('SELECT * FROM content_objects WHERE content_hash=?', hash);\n        if (old && (old.byte_size !== row.expected_size || old.storage_key !== key || !['READY','MISSING'].includes(String(old.state)))) reject('CONTENT_CONFLICT', 'Content metadata or deletion state conflicts');\n        if (old) exec(\"UPDATE content_objects SET state='READY',verified_at=?,last_error=NULL WHERE content_hash=?\", time, hash);\n        else exec(\"INSERT INTO content_objects(content_hash,byte_size,storage_key,mime_type,state,created_at,storage_provider,verified_at) VALUES(?,?,?,?,'READY',?,'local',?)\", hash, row.expected_size!, key, row.mime_type!, time, time);\n        const result = commitTarget(input.principalId, JSON.parse(String(row.target_json)), hash, row);\n        exec(\"UPDATE object_write_operations SET state='REGISTERED',error_json=NULL,updated_at=? WHERE write_id=?\", time, input.writeId);\n        exec(\"UPDATE operations SET status='SUCCEEDED',result_json=?,updated_at=? WHERE operation_id=?\", canonical({ ...result, writeId: input.writeId, contentHash: hash }), time, row.operation_id!);\n        return writeView(write(input.principalId, input.writeId));\n      });\n    },\n    failObjectWrite(input: SessionInput<'failObjectWrite'>) {\n      return transaction(db, () => {\n        const row = write(input.principalId, input.writeId);\n        if (row.state !== 'REGISTERED') exec(\"UPDATE object_write_operations SET state='FAILED',error_json=?,updated_at=? WHERE write_id=?\", canonical({ message: input.error }), now(), input.writeId);\n        return writeView(write(input.principalId, input.writeId));\n      });\n    },\n    scanObjectWrites(input: SessionInput<'scanObjectWrites'>) { return all(\"SELECT w.*,o.result_json FROM object_write_operations w JOIN operations o ON o.operation_id=w.operation_id WHERE write_id>? AND state<>'REGISTERED' ORDER BY write_id LIMIT ?\", input.afterWriteId, input.limit).map(writeView); },\n    ensureSession(input: SessionInput<'ensureSession'>) {\n      return transaction(db, () => {\n        const attempt = required(get('SELECT a.*,p.owner_id FROM attempts a JOIN runs r ON r.run_id=a.run_id JOIN projects p ON p.project_id=r.project_id WHERE a.attempt_id=? AND a.run_id=?', input.attemptId, input.runId));\n        if (attempt.owner_id !== input.principalId) reject('FORBIDDEN', 'Attempt belongs to another principal', 403);\n        const old = get('SELECT * FROM agent_sessions WHERE attempt_id=?', input.attemptId);\n        if (old) return sessionView(old);\n        if (attempt.ended_at !== null) reject('ATTEMPT_ENDED', 'Cannot start a session after the attempt ended');\n        const role = required(get('SELECT snapshot_id FROM role_snapshots WHERE role=? AND ended_at IS NULL', s.attempt_id!)) reject('ACTIVE_ATTEMPT_EXISTS', 'End the attempt before closing its session');\n        if (s.status !== 'CLOSED') exec(\"UPDATE agent_sessions SET status='CLOSED',closed_at=? WHERE session_id=?\", now(), input.sessionId);\n        return sessionView(session(input.principalId, input.sessionId));\n      });\n    },\n    listSessionMessages(input: SessionInput<'listSessionMessages'>) {\n      session(input.principalId, input.sessionId);\n      const messages = all('SELECT * FROM session_messages WHERE session_id=? AND message_seq>? AND step_no=?', input.sessionId, input.stepNo))); },\n    markModelStepSent(input: SessionInput<'markModelStepSent'>) {\n      return transaction(db, () => {\n        session(input.principalId, input.sessionId);\n        const step = required(get('SELECT * FROM model_steps WHERE session_id=? AND step_no=?', input.sessionId, input.stepNo)));\n      });\n    },\n    resolveContentRead(input: SessionInput<'resolveContentRead'>) {\n      return transaction(db, () => {\n        const content = resolveReference(input.principalId, input.reference), pinId = uid('pin');\n        exec(\"INSERT INTO storage_pins VALUES(?,?,'READ',?,?,?,'ACTIVE',?)\", pinId, content.content_hash!, input.principalId, canonical(input.reference), new Date(Date.now()+60000).toISOString(), now());\n        return { ...contentView(content), pinId };\n      });\n    },\n    releaseContentRead(input: SessionInput<'releaseContentRead'>) {\n      const result = exec(\"UPDATE storage_pins SET state='RELEASED' WHERE pin_id=? AND owner_id=? AND state='ACTIVE'\", input.pinId, input.principalId);\n      return { released: Number(result.changes) > 0 };\n    },\n    recoverContentReads() { return { released: Number(exec(\"UPDATE storage_pins SET state='RELEASED' WHERE owner_type='READ' AND state='ACTIVE'\").changes) }; },\n    listContentObjects(input: SessionInput<'listContentObjects'>) { return all('SELECT * FROM content_objects WHERE content_hash>? ORDER BY content_hash LIMIT ?', input.afterHash, input.limit).map(contentView); },\n    markContentMissing(input: SessionInput<'markContentMissing'>) { exec(\"UPDATE content_objects SET state='MISSING',last_error=? AND state='RELEASED'\", input.contentHash); exec('DELETE FROM content_objects WHERE content_hash=?', input.contentHash); return { contentHash: input.contentHash, deleted: true }; });\n    },\n  };\n unknown)(parsed);\n  } };\n}\n","import { randomUUID } from 'node:crypto';\nimport type { DatabaseSync, SQLInputValue } from 'node:sqlite';\nimport { assertResourceVersion, domainEvent, parseContract './input-protocol.js';\nimport type { InputMethod, InputParsed, UploadIntake, AttachmentView, WorkspaceView } from './input-protocol.js';\nimport type { Meta, OperationResult, RunView } from './protocol.js';\n\ntype Row=Record<string,string|number|null>;\ntype FactInput={principalId:string;projectId?:string|undefined;name:string;baseCommit:string;contentHash:string;kind:string;clientMessageId:string};\ninterface Helpers {\n StorageError(code,status,message);};\n\nexport function createInputDomain(db:DatabaseSync,h:Helpers){\n  const get=(sql:string,...args:SQLInputValue[])=>db.prepare(sql).get(...args) as Row|undefined;\n  const all=(sql:string,...args:SQLInputValue[])=>db.prepare(sql).all(...args) as Row[];\n  const exec=(sql:string,...args:SQLInputValue[])=>db.prepare(sql).run(...args);\n  const required=(row:Row|undefined)=>row??fail('RESOURCE_NOT_FOUND',404,'Resource does not exist or is not visible');\n  function authorize(principalId:string,projectId:string,write=false){\n    const WHERE operation_id=?\",canonical({errorCode:input.errorCode}),time,r.operation_id!);\n      return intakeView(intake(input.principalId,input.intakeId));\n    });},\n    recoverUploadIntakes(){return transaction(db,()=>{\n      let interrupted=0;\n      for(const r of all(\"SELECT * FROM upload_intakes WHERE state IN ('RECEIVING','VALIDATED')\")){\n        if(r.state==='VALIDATED'){\n          const a=get(\"SELECT attachment_id FROM attachments WHERE owner_id=? AND client_attachment_id=? AND content_hash=? AND name=? AND byte_size=? WHERE operation_id=?\",canonical({errorCode:'UPLOAD_INTERRUPTED'}),time,r.operation_id!);interrupted++;\n      }\n      return {interrupted};\n    });},\n  };\n  return './preparation-protocol.js';\n\nexport type PreparationRow=Record<string,string|number|null>;\nexport interface PreparationHelpers {\n preparationFail=(code:string,status:number,message:string):never=>{throw new StorageError(code,status,message);};\n\nexport function createUnderstandingDomain(db:DatabaseSync,h:PreparationHelpers){\n  const get=(sql:string,...args:SQLInputValue[])=>db.prepare(sql).get(...args) as PreparationRow|undefined;\n  const all=(sql:string,...args:SQLInputValue[])=>db.prepare(sql).all(...args) as Record<string,unknown>;\n  function authorize(principalId:string,runId:string,write=false){\n    const run=required(get('SELECT r.*,m.role AS membership FROM runs r JOIN project_members m ON WHERE r.run_id=?',principalId,runId));\n    if(write&&run.membership==='viewer')preparationFail('FORBIDDEN',403,'Project membership is read only');return run;\n  }\n  function jobView(row:PreparationRow):PreparationJob{return {jobId:String(row.job_id),runId:String(row.run_id),operationId:String(row.operation_id),principalId:String(row.principal_id),purpose:String(row.purpose),role:row.role as 'product'|'architect',state:String(row.state),basisHash:row.basis_hash as string|null,basis:row.basis_json===null?null:parse(row.basis_json),retryOf:row.retry_of as string|null,errorCode:row.error_code as string|null,resultArtifactId:row.result_artifact_id as string|null};}\n  const job=(jobId:string)=>required(get('SELECT * FROM preparation_jobs WHERE job_id=?',jobId));\n  const ready=(hash:string)=>required(get(\"SELECT * FROM content_objects WHERE content_hash=? AND state='READY'\",hash));\n  function newJob(operationId:string,run:PreparationRow,principalId:string,purpose:'UNDERSTAND'|'INTERPRET'|'PLAN',retryOf:string|null=null){\n    const id=preparationId('job'),time=now();\n    exec(\"INSERT INTO preparation_jobs(job_id,run_id,operation_id,principal_id,purpose,role,retry_of,created_at,updated_at) VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)\",id,run.run_id!,operationId,principalId,purpose,purpose==='PLAN'?'architect':'product',retryOf,time,time);\n    exec('INSERT OR IGNORE INTO input_operation_resources VALUES(?,?,?)',operationId,run.project_id!,run.run_id!);\n    return job(id);\n  }\n  function createProcessing(run:PreparationRow,principalId:string,purpose:'INTERPRET'|'PLAN',key:string){\n    const operationId=preparationId('op'),time=now();\n    exec(\"INSERT INTO operations VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,?,'WAITING',NULL,?,?)\",operationId,principalId,run.run_id!,key,digest(canonical({purpose,key})),purpose==='PLAN'?'PLAN_INPUT':'INTERPRET_ANSWERS',time,time);\n    const row=newJob(operationId,run,principalId,purpose);h.outbox(operationId,'understanding',{schemaVersion:1,runId:run.run_id,jobId:row.job_id});return row;\n  }\n  function sourceContext(row:PreparationRow):PreparationContext{\n    const run=authorize(String(row.principal_id),String(row.run_id));\n    const messages=all('SELECT m.message_id,m.body_artifact_id,a.content_hash,a.byte_size FROM input_messages m JOIN artifacts a ON a.artifact_id=m.body_artifact_id WHERE m.run_id=? AND revision=?',run.run_id!,run.current_requirement_revision!)).content_json);\n    const baseCommit=String(required(get('SELECT base_commit FROM user_tasks WHERE task_id=?',run.task_id!)).base_commit);\n    const basis={runId:run.run_id,requirementRevision:run.current_requirement_revision,baseCommit,sources:sources.map(s=>({ref:s.ref,contentHash:s.contentHash})),answerIds:answers.map(a=>a.answerId),answerInterventions:run.answer_interventions};\n    for(const a of all('SELECT b.path,a.artifact_id,a.content_hash,a.byte_size FROM preparation_bootstrap_sources b JOIN artifacts a ON a.artifact_id=b.artifact_id WHERE b.job_id=? WHERE job_id=?',context.basisHash,canonical(context.basis),now(),row.job_id!);return context;}\n  function sameBasis(row:PreparationRow){const context=sourceContext(row);if(row.basis_hash!==context.basisHash)preparationFail('BASIS_SUPERSEDED',409,'Preparation input changed');return context;}\n  function writable(principalId:string,runId:string,ifMatch?:string){const run=authorize(principalId,runId,true);assertResourceVersion(ifMatch,Number(run.resource_revision));if(run.terminal_outcome!==null||run.control_gate==='CANCELLING')preparationFail('RUN_NOT_WRITABLE',409,'Run cannot save answers');return run;}\n  function scope(meta:Meta,runId:string){if(meta.scopeId!==runId)preparationFail('OPERATION_SCOPE_INVALID',422,'Invalid Run scope');}\n  function assertPreparationPhase(run:PreparationRow){\n    if(!['PREPARING','UNDERSTANDING','PLANNING'].includes(String(run.phase))||run.terminal_outcome!==null||\n      get('SELECT 1 FROM plan_confirmations WHERE run_id=? (input:unknown)=>unknown)(parseContract(candidateIntegrationRequests[method],input));}};\n}\n","import type { DatabaseSync, SQLInputValue } from 'node:sqlite';\nimport { ids, inputManifest, parseContract, planDraft, productOutput } from '@devmesh/contracts';\nimport type { Draft } from '@devmesh/contracts';\nimport { StorageError } from './errors.js';\nimport { canonical } from './json.js';\nimport { digest } from './migrations.js';\nimport type { Parsed, ScheduledCandidate } from './protocol.js';\nimport type { createPlanDomain } from './plan-domain.js';\nimport type {createCandidateIntegrationDomain} from './candidate-integration-domain.js';\nimport {createPlanResults} from './plan-results.js';\n\ntype Row=Record<string,string|number|null>;\ntype Work=Draft['works'][number];\ntype Admission=Parsed<'admitScheduledWork'>;\nfunction reject(code:string,message:string,status=409):never{throw new StorageError(code,status,message);}\nconst json=(v:unknown)=>JSON.parse(String(v)) as unknown;\nexport function createSchedulerDomain(db:DatabaseSync,plans:ReturnType<typeof createPlanDomain>,candidates:ReturnType<typeof createCandidateIntegrationDomain>){\n  const planResults=createPlanResults(db);\n  const get=(sql:string,...args:SQLInputValue[])=>db.prepare(sql).get(...args) as Row|undefined;\n  const all=(sql:string,...args:SQLInputValue[])=>db.prepare(sql).all(...args) as Row[];\n  const exec=(sql:string,...args:SQLInputValue[])=>db.prepare(sql).run(...args);\n  const readyArtifact=(runId:string,ref:{artifactId:string;sha256:string})=>!!get(`SELECT 1 FROM artifacts a JOIN content_objects c ON c.content_hash=a.content_hash\n    WHERE a.run_id=? AND a.artifact_id=? AND a.content_hash=? AND a.state='READY' AND c.state='READY'`,runId,ref.artifactId,ref.sha256);\n  function workBasis(runId:string,planRevision:number,workId:string){\n    const row=get(`SELECT w.work_epoch,w.blocked_reason,rev.definition_json FROM plan_works p JOIN works w ON w.run_id=p.run_id AND w.work_id=p.work_id\n      JOIN work_revisions rev ON rev.run_id=p.run_id AND rev.work_id=p.work_id AND rev.revision=p.work_revision\n      WHERE p.run_id=? AND p.plan_revision=? AND p.work_id=?`,runId,planRevision,workId);\n    if(!row)reject('WORK_BASIS_CHANGED','Current plan no longer contains the work');\n    return row;\n  }\n  function dependencies(runId:string,planRevision:number,work:Work){\n    const results=new Map<string,Row>();\n    for(const edge of work.dependencies){\n      const result=planResults.resolve(runId,planRevision,edge.workId)?.row;\n      const verified=result&&(result.state==='SUCCEEDED'&&result.outcome==='success'||result.state==='FAILED'&&result.outcome==='completed-with-findings'&&edge.requiredOutcome==='completed-with-findings');\n      if(!verified||!result)return null;\n      results.set(edge.workId,result);\n    }\n    return results;\n  }\n  function freeRoles(runId:string){\n    return new Set(all('SELECT role FROM role_slots WHERE run_id=? UNION ALL SELECT role FROM preparation_slots WHERE run_id=?',runId,runId).map(r=>String(r.role)));\n  }\n  function hasAttempt(runId:string,work:Work){\n    return !!get('SELECT 1 FROM attempts WHERE run_id=? AND work_id=? AND (ended_at IS NULL OR work_revision=?)',runId,work.workId,work.workRevision);\n  }\n  function touch(projectId:string){\n    const turn=Number(get('SELECT MAX(turn) AS turn FROM scheduler_project_turns')?.turn??0)+1;\n    exec('INSERT INTO scheduler_project_turns VALUES(?,?) ON CONFLICT(project_id) DO UPDATE SET turn=excluded.turn',projectId,turn);\n  }\n  function defer(input:Parsed<'deferScheduledWork'>){\n    const row=get('SELECT project_id FROM runs WHERE run_id=? AND resource_revision=? AND terminal_outcome IS NULL AND control_gate=\\'OPEN\\'',input.runId,input.resourceRevision);\n    if(!row)return {deferred:false};\n    touch(String(row.project_id));return {deferred:true};\n  }\n  function next(input:Parsed<'nextScheduledWork'>):ScheduledCandidate[]{\n    const candidates:ScheduledCandidate[]=[];\n    // Bound each read. Only consider runs with at least one slot and unused Work.\n    const runs=all(`SELECT r.*,p.owner_id,p.updated_at,pr.content_json\n      FROM runs r JOIN projects p ON p.project_id=r.project_id\n      JOIN project_members m ON m.project_id=p.project_id AND m.principal_id=p.owner_id AND m.role='owner'\n      JOIN plan_revisions pr ON pr.run_id=r.run_id AND pr.revision=r.current_plan_revision AND LEFT JOIN scheduler_project_turns q ON q.project_id=r.project_id\n      LEFT JOIN scheduler_run_budget b ON b.run_id=r.run_id\n      WHERE r.control_gate='OPEN' AND r.terminal_outcome IS NULL\n      AND (b.run_id IS NULL OR b.admitted_attempts<b.maximum_attempts AND b.reserved_ms<b.maximum_reserved_ms)\n      AND (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM role_slots WHERE run_id=r.run_id)+(SELECT COUNT(*) FROM preparation_slots WHERE run_id=r.run_id)<2\n      AND EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM plan_works pw JOIN works w ON w.run_id=pw.run_id AND w.work_id=pw.work_id\n        WHERE pw.run_id=r.run_id AND a.work_revision=pw.work_revision)))\n      ORDER BY COALESCE(q.turn,0),r.created_at,r.run_id LIMIT ?`,Math.min(input.limit*4,400));\n    for(const row of runs){\n      const plans.pendingSource(runId,Number(row.current_requirement_revision))){touch(String(row.project_id));continue;}\n      const plan=parseContract(planDraft,json(row.content_json)),graph=plans.inspect(row,plan);\n      if(graph.issues.length){touch(String(row.project_id));continue;}\n      const items=new Map(plan.works.map(w=>[w.workId,w]));\n      const before=candidates.length;\n      for(const {workId} of graph.topology){\n        const work=items.get(workId)!;\n        if(occupied.has(work.role)||hasAttempt(runId,work)||!dependencies(runId,planRevision,work))continue;\n        const w=workBasis(runId,planRevision,workId);\n        if(w.blocked_reason!==null)continue;\n        candidates.push({projectId:String(row.project_id),runId,principalId:String(row.owner_id),runEpoch:Number(row.run_epoch),\n          resourceRevision:Number(row.resource_revision),planRevision,workEpoch:Number(w.work_epoch),work});\n        break;\n      }\n      if(candidates.length===before)touch(String(row.project_id));\n      if(candidates.length>=input.limit)break;\n    }\n    return candidates;\n  }\n  function currentManifest(input:Admission,run:Row){\n    const plan=get('SELECT content_json FROM plan_revisions WHERE run_id=? AND revision=?',input.runId,input.planRevision);\n    if(!plan||run.current_plan_revision!==input.planRevision)reject('PLAN_SUPERSEDED','Plan changed before scheduling');\n    if(!get('SELECT 1 FROM registry_control WHERE active_generation_id=?',input.work.endpoint.registryGenerationId))reject('REGISTRY_BINDING_CHANGED','Scheduler requires a committed endpoint generation');\n    const endpoint=get('SELECT config_json FROM endpoint_registrations WHERE endpoint_id=? AND revision=?',input.work.endpoint.endpointId,input.work.endpoint.revision);\n    const config=endpoint?json(endpoint.config_json) as {hosting?:string;allowedCallers?:string[];supportedSkills?:string[]}:null;\n    if(config?.hosting!=='local'||!config.allowedCallers?.includes(input.callerId)||!config.supportedSkills?.includes('devmesh.work.'+input.work.roleSnapshot.role))\n      reject('SCHEDULER_ENDPOINT_DENIED','Endpoint is not enabled for this service and role');\n    const draft=parseContract(planDraft,json(plan.content_json)),graph=plans.inspect(run,draft);\n    if(graph.issues.length)throw new StorageError('PLAN_INVALID',422,'Plan checks no longer pass',{issues:graph.issues.slice(0,100)});\n    const work=draft.works.find(w=>w.workId===input.work.workId);\n    if(!work||work.workRevision!==input.work.workRevision||hasAttempt(input.runId,work))reject('WORK_BASIS_CHANGED','Original work already has an attempt or changed revision');\n    const repair=get(\"SELECT basis_json,plan_json FROM verified_repairs WHERE run_id=? AND published_plan_revision=? AND state='PUBLISHED'\",input.runId,input.planRevision);\n    if(repair){const repairPlan=planDraft.parse(json(repair.plan_json));if(repairPlan.works.slice(-2).some(w=>w.workId===work.workId)){const basis=JSON.parse(String(repair.basis_json)) as {sourceAttemptId:string;carriedResults:{attemptId:string;checkConfigHash:string;templateVersion:string}[]},source=basis.carriedResults.find(f=>f.attemptId===basis.sourceAttemptId);if(!source||source.checkConfigHash!==input.checkConfigHash||source.templateVersion!==input.templateVersion)reject('REPAIR_POLICY_CHANGED','Repair admission must preserve the reviewed source policy and template');}}\n    const dep=dependencies(input.runId,input.planRevision,work),occupied=freeRoles(input.runId);\n    if(!dep)reject('DEPENDENCY_UNSATISFIED','Current predecessor has no verified outcome');\n    if(occupied.size>=2||occupied.has(work.role))reject('ROLE_CAPACITY_EXHAUSTED','Run role slots are occupied');\n    const prompt=get(`SELECT a.content_hash FROM artifacts a JOIN content_objects c ON c.content_hash=a.content_hash WHERE a.project_id=? AND a.state='READY' AND c.state='READY'`,run.project_id!,input.work.roleSnapshot.promptArtifactId);\n    if(!prompt)reject('ROLE_PROMPT_UNAVAILABLE','Role prompt must be a ready artifact in this project');\n    const refs=new Map<string,{artifactId:string;sha256:string}>();\n    for(const ref of work.inputRefs){\n      const source=ref.kind==='artifact'?ref:(json(dep.get(ref.workId)!.outputs_json) as {slot:string;ref:{artifactId:string;sha256:string}}[]).find(x=>x.slot===ref.outputSlot)?.ref;\n      if(!source||!readyArtifact(input.runId,source))reject('WORK_INPUT_UNAVAILABLE','A frozen Work input is unavailable');\n      refs.set(source.artifactId,{artifactId:source.artifactId,sha256:source.sha256});\n    }\n    const bases=new Set([...dep.values()].map(r=>r.code_commit as string|null).filter((x):x is string=>x!==null));\n    let codeCommit:string;\n    if(get(\"SELECT 1 FROM managed_repositories WHERE project_id=? AND state='READY'\",run.project_id!)){\n      // This executes within the existing admission transaction. Calling the\n      // public command here would open a nested SQLite transaction.\n      const subject={principalId:input.meta.principalId,runId:input.runId,purpose:'work-input' as const,workId:work.workId},basis=candidates.basis.freeze(subject);\n      if(work.role==='tester'&&basis.sources.length===0)reject('VERIFICATION_BASIS_UNAVAILABLE','Tester requires a verified development source');\n      const snapshot=basis.sources.length?candidates.qualified(subject):null;\n      if(snapshot&&(!snapshot.manifest||snapshot.purpose!=='work-input'||snapshot.workId!==work.workId||snapshot.basisSha256!==basis.sha256))reject('CANDIDATE_SNAPSHOT_UNAVAILABLE','Work requires its exact current dependency snapshot');\n      codeCommit=snapshot?.manifest?.commit??basis.baseCommit;\n    }else{\n      if(bases.size>1)reject('CODE_BASIS_AMBIGUOUS','Predecessor outputs require an integrated code snapshot');\n      codeCommit=[...bases][0]??String(get('SELECT base_commit FROM user_tasks WHERE task_id=?',run.task_id!)!.base_commit);\n      if(work.role==='tester'&&bases.size===0)reject('VERIFICATION_BASIS_UNAVAILABLE','Tester requires a verified code snapshot from its predecessor');\n    }\n    const req=get('SELECT content_json FROM requirement_revisions WHERE run_id=? AND revision=?',input.runId,draft.requirementRevision)!;\n    const requirements=parseContract(productOutput,json(req.content_json)).requirements;\n    const decision=get('SELECT decision_id FROM decisions WHERE run_id=? AND subject_kind=\\'PLAN\\' AND subject_id=?',input.runId,String(input.planRevision));\n    if(!decision)reject('PLAN_NOT_CONFIRMED','An explicit Plan decision is required');\n    const manifest=parseContract(inputManifest,{schemaVersion:'devmesh.input-manifest.v1',\n      requirements:requirements.filter(r=>work.requirementIds.includes(r.id)).map(r=>({requirementId:r.id,sha256:digest(canonical(r))})),\n      interfaces:[],artifacts:[...refs.values()],codeCommit,templateVersion:input.templateVersion,\n      checkConfigHash:input.checkConfigHash,decisionIds:[decision.decision_id]});\n    return {manifest,work,promptHash:String(prompt.content_hash)};\n  }\n  function reserve(input:Admission,run:Row,work:Work,promptHash:string){\n    const estimate=work.estimatedResources,limits=input.runnerLimits;\n    if(estimate.cpu>limits.cpu||estimate.memoryMiB>limits.memoryMiB||estimate.timeoutMs>limits.timeoutMs)\n      reject('RUNNER_RESOURCE_UNAVAILABLE','Work exceeds the configured Runner resource limits');\n    const capacity=Number(get(`SELECT COUNT(*) AS n FROM scheduled_work s JOIN attempts a ON a.attempt_id=s.attempt_id\n      WHERE s.runner_id=? AND a.ended_at IS NULL`,input.runnerId)?.n??0);\n    if(capacity>=input.runnerCapacity)reject('RUNNER_CAPACITY_EXHAUSTED','Runner capacity is occupied');\n    exec('INSERT OR IGNORE INTO scheduler_run_budget(run_id,maximum_attempts,maximum_reserved_ms) VALUES(?,?,?)',input.runId,input.maximumAttempts,input.maximumReservedMs);\n    const budget=get('SELECT * FROM scheduler_run_budget WHERE run_id=?',input.runId)!;\n    if(budget.maximum_attempts!==input.maximumAttempts||budget.maximum_reserved_ms!==input.maximumReservedMs)\n      reject('SCHEDULER_BUDGET_CHANGED','Current Run has a frozen admission budget');\n    if(Number(budget.admitted_attempts)>=input.maximumAttempts||Number(budget.reserved_ms)+work.estimatedResources.timeoutMs>input.maximumReservedMs)\n      reject('SCHEDULER_BUDGET_EXHAUSTED','Run admission budget is exhausted');\n    const {promptArtifactId:_prompt,...profile}=input.work.roleSnapshot;\n    const policyHash=digest(canonical({profile,promptHash,endpoint:input.work.endpoint,callerId:input.callerId,templateVersion:input.templateVersion,\n      checkConfigHash:input.checkConfigHash,runnerId:input.runnerId,runnerLimits:input.runnerLimits}));\n    exec('INSERT OR IGNORE INTO scheduler_run_roles VALUES(?,?,?)',input.runId,work.role,policyHash);\n    if(get('SELECT policy_hash FROM scheduler_run_roles WHERE run_id=? AND role=?',input.runId,work.role)?.policy_hash!==policyHash)\n      reject('SCHEDULER_ROLE_POLICY_CHANGED','Current Run role policy is frozen');\n    exec('UPDATE scheduler_run_budget SET admitted_attempts=admitted_attempts+1,reserved_ms=reserved_ms+? WHERE run_id=?',work.estimatedResources.timeoutMs,input.runId);\n    touch(String(run.project_id));\n  }\n  function verify(input:Parsed<'recordVerifiedWork'>){\n    const a=get('SELECT a.* FROM attempts a JOIN scheduled_work s ON s.attempt_id=a.attempt_id WHERE a.attempt_id=? null, attempt_id: owner.attemptId ?? null, seq: data.seq, operation_id: operationId, type, schema_version: 1, payload_json: canonical(payload), occurred_at: time, observed_at: time, time_source: 'observed', resource_revision: data.resourceRevision, requirement_revision: data.requirementRevision, plan_revision: data.planRevision });\n  }\n  function outbox(operationId: string, destination: string, payload: unknown) {\n    insert('outbox', { outbox_id: uid('outbox'), operation_id: operationId, message_no: Number(get('SELECT count(*) n FROM outbox WHERE operation_id=?', operationId)?.n ?? 0), destination, payload_json: canonical(payload), status: 'PENDING', delivery_attempts: 0, lease_owner: null, lease_until: null, next_attempt_at: now() });\n  }\n  function readyContent(hash: string) { return required(get(\"SELECT * FROM content_objects WHERE content_hash=? AND state='READY'\", hash)); }\n  function createInputFacts(input:{principalId:string;projectId?:string|undefined;name:string;baseCommit:string;contentHash:string;kind:string;clientMessageId:string}, operationId:string, legacyUnderstanding:boolean) {\n    const content=readyContent(input.contentHash),time=now();\n    const projectId=input.projectId??uid('prj'), taskId=uid('task'),runId=uid('run'),artifactId=uid('artifact'),messageId=uid('message');\n    if(!input.projectId){\n      insert('projects',{project_id:projectId,owner_id:input.principalId,name:input.name,revision:1,accepted_release_id:null,created_at:time,updated_at:time});\n      insert('project_members',{project_id:projectId,principal_id:input.principalId,role:'owner',created_at:time});\n    }\n    insert('user_tasks',{task_id:taskId,project_id:projectId,kind:input.kind,base_commit:input.baseCommit,request_artifact_id:artifactId,created_at:time});\n    insert('runs',{run_id:runId,project_id:projectId,task_id:taskId,phase:'UNDERSTANDING',created_at:time});\n    insert('artifacts',{artifact_id:artifactId,project_id:projectId,run_id:runId,attempt_id:null,kind:'USER_INPUT',content_hash:input.contentHash,byte_size:Number(content.byte_size),storage_key:String(content.storage_key),state:'READY',created_at:time});\n    insert('input_messages',{message_id:messageId,project_id:projectId,run_id:runId,author:input.principalId,body_artifact_id:artifactId,client_message_id:input.clientMessageId,created_at:time});\n    event(runId,operationId,'InputSaved',{messageId});\n    if(legacyUnderstanding)outbox(operationId,'understanding',{schemaVersion:1,projectId,runId,messageId});\n    exec('UPDATE projects SET updated_at=? WHERE project_id=?',time,projectId);\n    return {projectId,taskId,runId,artifactId,messageId,revision:1};\n  }\n  const inputs=createInputDomain(db,{operation,createInputFacts,event,outbox,runView});\n  function publishPlanFacts(current:Row,requirementRevision:number,planRevision:number,plan:Parsed<'publishPlan'>['plan'],planHash:string,operationId:string){\n    const runId=String(current.run_id),time=now(),planContent=readyContent(planHash),artifactId=uid('artifact');\n    insert('plan_revisions',{run_id:runId,revision:planRevision,requirement_revision:requirementRevision,content_hash:planHash,content_json:canonical(plan),created_at:time});\n    for(const work of plan.works){\n      exec('INSERT OR IGNORE INTO works(run_id,work_id) VALUES(?,?)',runId,work.workId);\n      const previous=get('SELECT definition_hash FROM work_revisions WHERE run_id=? AND work_id=? AND revision=?',runId,work.workId,work.workRevision),hash=digest(canonical(work));\n      if(previous&&previous.definition_hash!==hash)throw new StorageError('WORK_REVISION_IMMUTABLE',409,'Published work definition cannot change');\n      if(!previous)insert('work_revisions',{run_id:runId,work_id:work.workId,revision:work.workRevision,role:work.role,definition_json:canonical(work),definition_hash:hash});\n      insert('plan_works',{run_id:runId,plan_revision:planRevision,work_id:work.workId,work_revision:work.workRevision});\n    }\n    for(const work of plan.works)for(const dependency of work.dependencies)insert('plan_edges',{run_id:runId,plan_revision:planRevision,predecessor:dependency.workId,successor:work.workId,required_outcome:dependency.requiredOutcome});\n    exec(\"UPDATE runs SET phase='PLANNING',current_requirement_revision=?,current_plan_revision=? WHERE run_id=?\",requirementRevision,planRevision,runId);bump(runId);\n    insert('artifacts',{artifact_id:artifactId,project_id:String(current.project_id),run_id:runId,attempt_id:null,kind:'PLAN',content_hash:planHash,byte_size:Number(planContent.byte_size),storage_key:String(planContent.storage_key),state:'READY',created_at:time});\n    event(runId,operationId,'PlanPublished',{planRevision,planRef:{artifactId,sha256:planHash}});\n    return {artifactId,sha256:planHash,runId,planRevision,requirementRevision,reviewHash:planHash,revision:Number(run(runId).resource_revision)};\n  }\n  const preparations=createPreparationDomain(db,{operation,event,outbox,publishPlanFacts});\n  const dispatch=createDispatchDomain(db,(runId,operationId,workId,attemptId,state)=>{\n    bump(runId);event(runId,operationId,'AttemptStateChanged',{attemptId,state},{workId,attemptId});\n  });\n  function confirm(input:Parsed<'confirmPlan'>,strict:boolean){\n    const {meta,ifMatch:_ifMatch,...payload}=input;\n    if(meta.scopeId!==input.runId)throw new StorageError('OPERATION_SCOPE_INVALID',422,'Invalid Run scope');\n    return operation(meta,'ConfirmPlan',payload,operationId=>{\n      const current=writable(input);\n      if(!['OPEN','PAUSED'].includes(String(current.control_gate)))throw new StorageError('ADMISSION_CLOSED',409,'The Run is stopping');\n      const plan=required(get('SELECT * FROM plan_revisions WHERE run_id=? AND revision=?',input.runId,input.planRevision));\n      if(current.current_plan_revision!==input.planRevision||current.current_requirement_revision!==input.requirementRevision||plan.content_hash!==input.reviewHash||plan.requirement_revision!==input.requirementRevision)\n        throw new StorageError('PLAN_SUPERSEDED',409,'The reviewed plan is no longer current',{currentPlanRevision:current.current_plan_revision,currentRequirementRevision:current.current_requirement_revision});\n      if(get('SELECT 1 FROM plan_confirmations WHERE run_id=? AND plan_revision=?',input.runId,input.planRevision))\n        throw new StorageError('PLAN_ALREADY_CONFIRMED',409,'This plan already has a decision');\n      if(plans.pendingQuestions(input.runId))throw new StorageError('REQUIRED_QUESTIONS_UNANSWERED',409,'Required questions must be answered before implementation');\n      if(plans.pendingInterpretation(input.runId,input.requirementRevision))throw new StorageError('INTERPRETATION_PENDING',409,'Saved answers must be interpreted before plan confirmation');\n      if(plans.pendingSource(input.runId,input.requirementRevision))throw new StorageError('BASIS_SUPERSEDED',409,'Saved source input has not been processed into this plan');\n      if(strict||get('SELECT 1 FROM understanding_revisions WHERE run_id=? AND requirement_revision=?',input.runId,input.requirementRevision)){\n        const graph=plans.inspect(current,parseContract(planDraft,JSON.parse(String(plan.content_json))));\n        if(graph.issues.length)throw new StorageError('PLAN_INVALID',422,'Plan checks must pass before confirmation',{issues:graph.issues.slice(0,100)});\n      }\n      const repair=verifiedRepairs.assertConfirmation(input.runId,input.planRevision,input.carryForwardHash);\n      insert('plan_confirmations',{run_id:input.runId,plan_revision:input.planRevision,requirement_revision:input.requirementRevision,review_hash:input.reviewHash,actor:meta.principalId,operation_id:operationId,created_at:now()});\n      const decisionId=uid('decision');\n      insert('decisions',{decision_id:decisionId,run_id:input.runId,subject_id:String(input.planRevision),subject_kind:'PLAN',basis_hash:input.reviewHash,choice_json:canonical({confirmed:true,...(repair?{carryForwardHash:input.carryForwardHash}:{})}),operation_id:operationId,actor_id:meta.principalId,created_at:now()});\n      if(repair)verifiedFindings.linkApprovedRepair(current,repair.issueIds,repair.fixWorkId);\n      insert('input_operation_resources',{operation_id:operationId,project_id:current.project_id!,run_id:input.runId});\n      bump(input.runId);\n      event(input.runId,operationId,'PlanConfirmed',{planRevision:input.planRevision,decisionId});\n      if(current.control_gate==='OPEN')outbox(operationId,'scheduler',{schemaVersion:1,runId:input.runId,planRevision:input.planRevision});\n      return {projectId:current.project_id,runId:input.runId,planRevision:input.planRevision,revision:Number(run(input.runId).resource_revision)};\n    },()=>{plans.authorized(meta.principalId,input.runId,true);});\n  }\n  function knownEffects(attemptId: string) {\n    return knownAttemptEffects(db,attemptId);\n  }\n  function evidence(ref: { artifactId: string; sha256: string } | undefined, runId: string) {\n    if (!ref || !get(\"SELECT artifact_id FROM artifacts WHERE artifact_id=? AND run_id=? AND content_hash=? AND state='READY'\", ref.artifactId, runId, ref.sha256)) throw new StorageError('RECONCILIATION_EVIDENCE_REQUIRED', 409, 'A persisted evidence artifact belonging to the Run is required');\n  }\n\n  function admitWorkFacts(input:Parsed<'admitWorks'>,operationId:string){\n    const {meta}=input;\n    const current = writable(input);\n    if (current.control_gate !== 'OPEN' || current.run_epoch !== input.runEpoch) throw new StorageError('ADMISSION_CLOSED', 409, 'Run gate or epoch changed');\n    if (current.current_plan_revision !== input.planRevision || !get('SELECT run_id FROM plan_confirmations WHERE run_id=? AND plan_revision=? AND requirement_revision=?', input.runId, input.planRevision,current.current_requirement_revision??null)) throw new StorageError('PLAN_NOT_CONFIRMED', 409, 'Current plan and requirement basis must be explicitly confirmed');\n    const attemptIds: string[] = [];\n    for (const requested of input.works) {\n      const work = required(get('SELECT w.work_epoch,w.blocked_reason,r.role,r.definition_json FROM plan_works p JOIN works w ON w.run_id=p.run_id AND w.work_id=p.work_id JOIN work_revisions r ON r.run_id=p.run_id AND r.work_id=p.work_id AND r.revision=p.work_revision WHERE p.run_id=? AND p.plan_revision=? AND p.work_id=? AND p.work_revision=?', input.runId, input.planRevision, requested.workId, requested.workRevision));\n      if (work.work_epoch !== requested.workEpoch || work.blocked_reason !== null || work.role !== requested.roleSnapshot.role) throw new StorageError('WORK_BASIS_CHANGED', 409, 'Work epoch, role or freeze changed');\n      if (get('SELECT attempt_id FROM attempts WHERE run_id=? AND work_id=? AND ended_at IS NULL', input.runId, requested.workId)) throw new StorageError('ACTIVE_ATTEMPT_EXISTS', 409, 'Original attempt must be reconciled/ended before retry');\n      for (const edge of all('SELECT predecessor,required_outcome FROM plan_edges WHERE run_id=? AND plan_revision=? AND successor=?', input.runId, input.planRevision, requested.workId)) {\n        const previous = get('SELECT state FROM attempts WHERE run_id=? AND work_id=? ORDER BY attempt_no DESC LIMIT 1', input.runId, String(edge.predecessor));\n        if (!previous || (previous.state !== 'SUCCEEDED' && !(edge.required_outcome === 'completed-with-findings' && previous.state === 'FAILED'))) throw new StorageError('DEPENDENCY_UNSATISFIED', 409, 'Predecessor has no required verified outcome');\n      }\n      const attemptId = uid('attempt'), activationId = uid('activation'), grantId = uid('grant'), dispatchId = uid('op'), time = now();\n      const number = Number(get('SELECT max(attempt_no) n FROM attempts WHERE run_id=? AND work_id=?', input.runId, requested.workId)?.n ?? 0) + 1;\n      const fingerprint = digest(canonical(requested.inputManifest));\n      if (requested.inputManifest.requirements.length === 0) throw new StorageError('INPUT_MANIFEST_EMPTY', 422, 'Implementation work must reference requirements');\n      const requirementData = JSON.parse(String(required(get('SELECT content_json FROM requirement_revisions WHERE run_id=? AND revision=?', input.runId, Number(current.current_requirement_revision))).content_json)) as { requirements: { id: string }[] };\n      const hashes = new Map(requirementData.requirements.map(requirement => [requirement.id, digest(canonical(requirement))]));\n      if (requested.inputManifest.requirements.some(ref => hashes.get(ref.requirementId) !== ref.sha256)) throw new StorageError('INPUT_BASIS_CHANGED', 409, 'Input requirement fingerprints differ from the current snapshot');\n      const definition = JSON.parse(String(work.definition_json)) as { requirementIds: string[] };\n      const included = new Set<string>(requested.inputManifest.requirements.map(ref => ref.requirementId));\n      if (definition.requirementIds.some(id => !included.has(id))) throw new StorageError('INPUT_REQUIREMENTS_MISSING', 409, 'Input manifest omits requirements assigned to this work');\n      insert('operations', { operation_id: dispatchId, principal_id: meta.principalId, scope_id: input.runId, idempotency_key: uid('dispatch'), request_hash: digest(canonical(requested)), kind: 'DISPATCH', status: 'ACCEPTED', result_json: null, created_at: time, updated_at: time });\n      insert('attempts', { attempt_id: attemptId, run_id: input.runId, work_id: requested.workId, work_revision: requested.workRevision, role: String(work.role), attempt_no: number, state: 'RESERVED', admitted_run_epoch: input.runEpoch, admitted_work_epoch: requested.workEpoch, input_manifest_json: canonical(requested.inputManifest), input_fingerprint: fingerprint, role_snapshot_json: canonical(requested.roleSnapshot), dispatch_operation_id: dispatchId, created_at: time });\n      const snapshotId = uid('role');\n      insert('role_snapshots', { snapshot_id: snapshotId, role: String(work.role), revision: requested.roleSnapshot.revision, snapshot_json: canonical(requested.roleSnapshot), content_hash: digest(canonical(requested.roleSnapshot)), created_at: time });\n      const permit = parseContract(executionPermit, { schemaVersion: 'devmesh.permit.v1', ownerKind: 'workflowAttempt', runId: input.runId, runEpoch: input.runEpoch, workId: requested.workId, workEpoch: requested.workEpoch, attemptId, grantId, activationId, notBefore: time, expiresAt: new Date(Date.parse(time)+15000).toISOString(), resources: requested.resources });\n      insert('execution_grants', { grant_id: grantId, run_id: input.runId, attempt_id: attemptId, activation_id: activationId, permit_json: canonical(permit), status: 'ACTIVE', created_at: time, expires_at: permit.expiresAt });\n      insert('role_slots', { run_id: input.runId, role: String(work.role), attempt_id: attemptId, activation_id: activationId });\n      const inputManifestId=registry.freezeDispatch(attemptId,String(work.role),requested.endpoint,fingerprint,snapshotId);\n      outbox(dispatchId, requested.endpoint.endpointId, { schemaVersion: 'devmesh.dispatch-intent.v1', operationId: dispatchId, runId: input.runId, attemptId, workId: requested.workId, workRevision: requested.workRevision, endpoint: requested.endpoint, roleSnapshotId: snapshotId, permit, inputFingerprint: fingerprint, ...(inputManifestId?{inputManifestId}:{}) });\n      bump(input.runId);\n      event(input.runId, operationId, 'WorkAdmitted', { workId: requested.workId, attemptId, activationId }, { workId: requested.workId, attemptId });\n      attemptIds.push(attemptId);\n    }\n    exec(\"UPDATE runs SET phase='EXECUTING' WHERE run_id=?\", input.runId);\n    return { runId: input.runId, attemptIds, revision: Number(run(input.runId).resource_revision) };\n  }\n  const handlers = {\n    getCandidateSourceBasis(input:Parsed<'getCandidateSourceBasis'>){return transaction(db,()=>candidateBasis.freeze(input));},\n    assertCandidateSourceBasis(input:Parsed<'assertCandidateSourceBasis'>){return transaction(db,()=>{\n      const current=candidateBasis.freeze(input);\n      if(current.sha256!==input.basisSha256)throw input.contentHash);\n        if (previous && (previous.byte_size !== input.byteSize || previous.state !== 'READY')) throw new StorageError('CONTENT_CONFLICT', 409, 'Content size/state differs');\n        if (!previous) insert('content_objects', { content_hash: input.contentHash, byte_size: input.byteSize, storage_key: storageKey, mime_type: input.mimeType, state: 'READY', created_at: now() });\n        return { contentHash: input.contentHash, storageKey };\n      });\n    },\n    createProjectWithRun(input: Parsed<'createProjectWithRun'>) {\n      const { meta, ...payload } = input;\n  const {runId,planRevision,requirementRevision,reviewHash,revision}=publishPlanFacts(current,input.requirementRevision,input.planRevision,input.plan,planHash,operationId);\n        return reconcileScheduledDispatch(input:Parsed<'reconcileScheduledDispatch'>){return transaction(db,()=>dispatch.reconcile(input));},\n    admitScheduledWork(input:Parsed<'admitScheduledWork'>){\n      const if(current.run_epoch!==input.runEpoch||current.control_gate!=='OPEN'||current.current_plan_revision!==input.planRevision||\n          !get('SELECT 1 FROM plan_confirmations WHERE run_id=? AND plan_revision=? AND requirement_revision=?',input.runId,input.planRevision,current.current_requirement_revision??null))\n          throw new StorageError('ADMISSION_CLOSED',409,'Run or confirmed plan changed before scheduling');\n        if(plans.pendingQuestions(input.runId)||plans.pendingInterpretation(input.runId,Number(current.current_requirement_revision))||\n          plans.pendingSource(input.runId,Number(current.current_requirement_revision)))\n          throw new StorageError('ADMISSION_CLOSED',409,'Run still requires clarification');\n        const        return result;\n      },()=>{plans.authorized(meta.principalId,input.runId,true);});\n    },\n    recordVerifiedWork(input:Parsed<'recordVerifiedWork'>){\n      const {meta,...payload}=input;\n      if(meta.scopeId!==input.runId)throw new StorageError('OPERATION_SCOPE_INVALID',422,'Invalid verification scope');\n      return operation(meta,'RecordVerifiedWork',payload,operationId=>{\n        const current=run(input.runId);\n        if(current.terminal_outcome!==null||current.control_gate!=='OPEN')throw new StorageError('ADMISSION_CLOSED',409,'Run does not accept a verification result');\n AND run_id=?', input.attemptId, input.runId));\n        const current = run(input.runId);\n        if (attempt.ended_at !== null || current.terminal_outcome !== null || attempt.state !== input.expectedState) throw new StorageError('ATTEMPT_STATE_CONFLICT', 409, 'Attempt ended or expected state changed');\n        const transitions: Record<string, string[]> = { RESERVED: ['SUBMITTED','STOPPING','OUTCOME_UNKNOWN','FAILED'], SUBMITTED: ['WORKING','STOPPING','OUTCOME_UNKNOWN','FAILED'], WORKING: ['INPUT_REQUIRED','VERIFYING','STOPPING','OUTCOME_UNKNOWN','FAILED'], INPUT_REQUIRED: ['WORKING','STOPPING','OUTCOME_UNKNOWN'], VERIFYING: ['SUCCEEDED','FAILED','STOPPING','OUTCOME_UNKNOWN'], OUTCOME_UNKNOWN: ['SUBMITTED','WORKING','INPUT_REQUIRED','VERIFYING','STOPPING','FAILED','INTERRUPTED','CANCELLED'], STOPPING: ['INTERRUPTED','CANCELLED','OUTCOME_UNKNOWN'] };\n        if (!transitions[String(attempt.state)]?.includes(input.state)) throw new StorageError('ATTEMPT_TRANSITION_INVALID', 409, 'Illegal state transition');\n        if (input.state === 'WORKING' && (current.control_gate !== 'OPEN' || current.run_epoch !== attempt.admitted_run_epoch || get('SELECT work_epoch FROM works WHERE run_id=? AND work_id=?', input.runId, String(attempt.work_id))?.work_epoch !== attempt.admitted_work_epoch || !get(\"SELECT grant_id FROM execution_grants WHERE attempt_id=? AND status='ACTIVE' AND expires_at>?\", input.attemptId, now()))) throw new StorageError('ADMISSION_CLOSED', 409, 'Cannot resume work under a revoked or expired grant/epoch');\n        const terminal = ['SUCCEEDED','FAILED','INTERRUPTED','CANCELLED'].includes(input.state);\n        if (attempt.state === 'OUTCOME_UNKNOWN') evidence(input.evidenceRef, input.runId);\n        if (terminal && !knownEffects(input.attemptId)) throw new StorageError('EFFECT_OUTCOME_UNKNOWN', 409, 'Original tool/job effects must be reconciled');\n        exec('UPDATE attempts SET state=?,ended_at=? WHERE attempt_id=?', input.state, terminal ? now() : null, input.attemptId);\n        if (terminal) {\n          exec('DELETE FROM role_slots WHERE attempt_id=?', input.attemptId);\n          exec(\"UPDATE execution_grants SET status='REVOKED' WHERE attempt_id=?\", input.attemptId);\n        }\n        bump(input.runId);\n        event(input.runId, operationId, 'AttemptStateChanged', { attemptId: input.attemptId, state: input.state }, { workId: String(attempt.work_id), attemptId: input.attemptId });\n        return { attemptId: input.attemptId, state: input.state, revision: Number(run(input.runId).resource_revision) };\n      });\n    },\n    setControlIntent(input: Parsed<'setControlIntent'>) {\n      const { meta, ...payload } = input;\n      return operation(meta, 'SetControlIntent', payload, operationId => {\n        const current = writable(input);\n        if (current.control_gate === 'CANCELLING' && input.action === 'pause') throw new StorageError('CANCEL_INTENT_SAVED', 409, 'Pause cannot overwrite cancellation');\n        const active=get('SELECT attempt_id FROM attempts WHERE run_id=? AND ended_at IS NULL', input.runId)) throw new StorageError('ACTIVE_ATTEMPT_EXISTS', 409, 'All attempts must be known and ended');\n        if(get('SELECT preparation_attempt_id FROM preparation_attempts WHERE run_id=?
